  816 |    * defect belonging to a different story's ATC never bleeds into this
  817 |    * story's chain even when the underlying Test/Run row is shared. A
  818 |    * `story-traceability-isolation.test.ts` DB-integration suite (11/11
  819 |    * passing) proves the SQL predicate in isolation; this ATC is the only
  820 |    * proof the live UI actually respects that scoping boundary end-to-end -
  821 |    * a regression here would be a CRITICAL-severity cross-story data leak.
  822 |    *
  823 |    * @param args - the Project slug, this Story, and the shared ATC/foreign defect identity
  824 |    * @param args.projectSlug - Project slug the Story belongs to
  825 |    * @param args.userStoryId - this Story, whose own ATC shares the Run
  826 |    * @param args.ownAtcId - this Story's own ATC in the shared Run
  827 |    * @param args.foreignDefectTitle - the foreign Story's defect title, asserted absent everywhere
  828 |    */
  829 |   @atc('BK-460')
  830 |   async expectSharedRunHidesForeignDefect(
  831 |     args: { projectSlug: string, userStoryId: string, ownAtcId: string, foreignDefectTitle: string },
  832 |   ): Promise<void> {
  833 |     await this.goto(args);
  834 | 
  835 |     const ownAtcRow = this.page.locator(`[data-testid="traceability-atc-row-${args.ownAtcId}"]`);
  836 |     await expect(ownAtcRow).toBeVisible();
  837 |     await expect(ownAtcRow.locator('span.truncate.text-fg-2').first()).not.toHaveText('');
  838 | 
  839 |     await expect(this.page.getByText(args.foreignDefectTitle, { exact: true })).toHaveCount(0);
  840 |   }
  841 | 
  842 |   /**
  843 |    * ATC: Open a Story hosting an ATC missing a layer downstream of itself
  844 |    * (no Test chained, a Test chained but zero Runs ever started, or a Run
  845 |    * with zero linked Defects) - expects the exact layer-specific
  846 |    * "awaiting data" copy in the relevant cell(s), never a null or blank
  847 |    * cell (BK-45, TC-BK45-09 / BK-461).
  848 |    *
  849 |    * CORRECTED SCOPE (2026-08-25): the TC's Jira Examples table listed 5 rows
  850 |    * under one ambiguous "missing_layer" label, three of which all said
  851 |    * "Run" with three different, seemingly contradictory copies. Confirmed
  852 |    * live against staging that this was a labeling problem, not a real
  853 |    * product ambiguity - the 3 "Run" rows actually span TWO different CELLS
  854 |    * (Run and Defect) across the SAME TWO real preconditions:
  855 |    * - No Test chained at all: Test cell = "No test written yet", Run cell
  856 |    *   = "Awaiting test", Defect cell = "Awaiting test" too.
  857 |    * - A Test chained, zero Runs ever started: Run cell = "No run recorded
  858 |    *   yet", Defect cell = "Awaiting first run" (this is the Jira table's
  859 |    *   mislabeled 4th "Run" row - it is actually the Defect cell's
  860 |    *   placeholder for this precondition, not a second Run-cell copy).
  861 |    * A Run existing with zero linked Defects ("None linked") was already
  862 |    * confirmed as its own, unambiguous case - not part of the corrected
  863 |    * scope above.
  864 |    *
  865 |    * Deliberately a single flexible method rather than one method per cell:
  866 |    * each precondition below only has meaningful copy in a subset of the 3
  867 |    * cells, so the caller passes only the cell(s) that precondition
  868 |    * populates, per Test file below.
  869 |    *
  870 |    * @param args - the Project slug, Story, target ATC, and whichever cell copy applies to it
  871 |    * @param args.projectSlug - Project slug the Story belongs to
  872 |    * @param args.userStoryId - the Story hosting the target ATC
  873 |    * @param args.atcId - the ATC missing the layer(s) under test
  874 |    * @param args.expectedTestCopy - expected Test-cell copy, when the ATC has no Test chained
  875 |    * @param args.expectedRunCopy - expected Run-cell copy, when relevant
  876 |    * @param args.expectedDefectCopy - expected Defect-cell copy, when relevant
  877 |    */
  878 |   @atc('BK-461')
  879 |   async expectLayerGapCopy(
  880 |     args: {
  881 |       projectSlug: string
  882 |       userStoryId: string
  883 |       atcId: string
  884 |       expectedTestCopy?: string
  885 |       expectedRunCopy?: string
  886 |       expectedDefectCopy?: string
  887 |     },
  888 |   ): Promise<void> {
  889 |     await this.goto(args);
  890 | 
  891 |     const atcRow = this.page.locator(`[data-testid="traceability-atc-row-${args.atcId}"]`);

  893 |

  899 |     if (args.expectedRunCopy !== undefined) {

  902 |     if (args.expectedDefectCopy !== undefined) {

  904 |     }
  905 |   }
